def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
  t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10,float(budget_s)); hard=t0+budget
  deadline=hard - max(10,0.03*budget)  # finish by hard
  get parent
  best = parent normalized; best_score = exact/internal
  cache exact counts
  rng
  helper try_candidate
  # If budget small, just coordinate polish and return
  lengths = [256 if parent else 256, 512, 1024, 384?] based on budget.
  # Phase A: coarse initialization search at L=256 (and maybe 128) for 25% budget
  # Generate inits, evaluate, select ones close to best.
  # For each selected, run _hbfgs_search with betas [5,15,40,100,250,600,1500] maxfun scaled, using stop.
  # Phase B: main refine at best length and one larger length for 50%
  # Phase C: random restarts at lengths 256/512 for 20%
  # Phase D: coordinate polish 10%
  return finalize
```

Implement _lbfgs_smooth(z, anchor, beta, maxfun, stop):
```
def func(v):
  if time>stop: raise Timeout
  a = softmax_anchored(v, anchor)
  c = conv_same(a)
  M = c.max()
  if M<=0: return 0, zeros
  zc = beta*(c-M); np.exp; s
  F = M + log(s)/beta
  if not finite:F return large
  d = expz/s
  g_a = 2*valid_conv(d,a)
  dot = a@g_a
  ygrad = a*(g_a - dot)
  ygrad[anchor]=0? return F, ygrad without anchor
```

Pseudo:

def _smooth_refine(a0, stop, maxfun_total=1500, max_betas=5, min_rem=2.0):
 a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
 best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(a)
 if n<2 or stop-time <=1: return best,best_sc
 betas=[]
 b=max(4.0, n/8.0)
 for _ in range(max_betas): betas.append(min(b, 100000.0)); b*=4.0
 per = max(40, maxfun_total//len(betas))
 for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
   remaining=stop - time.monotonic()
   if remaining<10: break
   mf=min(per, int(remaining*20))? caps
   if mf<30: break
   anchor=int(np.argmax(best))
   v = _to_z(best, anchor)
   def fg(vv, ...): ...
   v0=np.clip(v0, -40,40)
   try res=minimize(fg, v0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bnds, options={...})
   except Timeout: pass
   # evaluate candidate at res.x and maybe v0
   for vv in (v0, getattr(res,'x',None)):
      a_c=_softmax_anchored(vv, anchor); sc=_score_internal(a_c); if sc<best_sc: best=a_c; best_sc=sc
   # optional if no improvement and beta high break? But keep trying.
 return best,best_sc
```

Build A matrix efficiently for n up to 320:
```
def _lp_direction(f, M):
 n=len(f); rows=2*n-1
 A=np.zeros((rows,n))
 for k in range(rows):
   i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
   # columns j=k-i, i from i0 to i1; j range max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k)
   j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(n-1,k)
   # A[k, j] = f[k-j]
   js = np.arange(j0,j1+1)
   A[k, js] = f[k-js]
```
For k=0: j0=0,j1=0 A[0,0]=f[0]. k=n-1: j0=0,j1=n-1: A[n-1,j]=f[n-1-j] reversed f. Good.
`linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(rows,M), bounds=[(0,1)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})`
Status 0. x = res.x.
If sum x <=0: return None. Normalize x.

def _line_search(f,g):
 cf=conv_same(f); cg=conv_same(g); cfs=conv_cross(f,g)
 base=cf; b=2*(cfs-cf); c=cf-2*cfs+cg
 ts=np.linspace(0,1,2048)
 vals=np.max(base + b*ts[:,None] + c*ts[:,None]**2, axis=1)
 j=argmin; maybe refine local.
 return ts[j]
```

def _z_from_a(a, anchor):
  base=max(float(a[anchor]),1e-300)
  z=np.empty(a.size-1)
  idx=0
  for i in range(a.size):
    if i!=anchor:
      z[idx]=math.log(max(float(a[i]),1e-300)/base)
      idx+=1
  return np.clip(z, -40, 40)

```
def fg(v, anchor=anchor, n=n):
  if tt>stop: raise _Timeout
  v=np.asarray(v)
  z=np.empty(n); ...
  m=z.max(); ex=np.exp(z-m); Z=ex.sum()
  if not finite: return 1e100, zeros(n-1)
  a=ex/Z
  c=_conv_same(a); M=float(c.max())
  if M<=1e-300: return 0, zeros
  zc=beta*(c-M); zc=np.clip(zc, -745, 0)
  w=np.exp(zc); s=w.sum()
  if s<=0: w = np.ones(2n-1)/(2n-1); s=...
  F = M + math.log(s)/beta
  d=w/s
  ga=2.0*_valid_conv(d,a)
  if not all finite: ...
  dot=float(a@ga)
  gf=a*(ga-dot)
  grad=np.empty(n-1); grad[:anchor]=gf[:anchor]; grad[anchor:]=gf[anchor+1:]
  grad=np.where(finite,grad,0)
  return float(F), grad
```
If `beta` huge and `M` small, `math.log(s)` finite. If s maybe 1, log0. Good.

def _process_length(L, stop, init_extra=None, per_beta=160, beta_count=4, max_trials=3):
  global best...
  inits = _make_inits(L, parent, best_seq, rng)
  # score them
  scored=[]
  for a in inits:
    sc=_score_internal(a)
    if finite: scored.append((sc,a))
  scored.sort()
  # If best_seq at same L already, include? inits includes best interpolation maybe.
  # We rank. For each top up to max_trials, allocate time.
  deadline=stop
  for trial,a in enumerate(scored[:max_trials]):
     if time > deadline-3: break
     # allocate share = (deadline - now)/(remaining_trials)
     remaining_trials = max_trials - trial
     substop = min(deadline - 5, time.monotonic() + (deadline - time.monotonic())/remaining_trials * 0.9)
     if substop - now < 5: break
     # If score already too poor relative best and not close, maybe skip
     if sc > best_score + 0.3 and trial>0: break
     arr,_ = _smooth_refine(a, substop, per_beta=per_beta, beta_count=beta_count)
     _try_candidate(arr)
  # coordinate polish local best maybe best_seq if length L? Use arr best local.
  # For simplicity, after _smooth_refine, if time>stop-20 and best_seq length L: coordinate polish? Better do final global coordinate.
```

Coordinate polish function:
```
def _coordinate_polish(a0, stop, seed=1, max_passes=2, mults=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5,2.0,3.0)):
  a=_normalize(a0).copy()
  n=a.size
  best_sc=_score_internal(a)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    improved=False
    order = rng.permutation(n)  # or sorted by a? random better
    default = 1.0/(4.0*n)
    for i in order:
      if time>stop: break
      orig=float(a[i])
      vals=set()
      vals.add(orig); vals.add(0.0)
      vals.add(default)
      vals.add(default*2); vals.add(default*4)
      for m in mults: vals.add(orig*m)
      # line search if orig > default:
      bestv=orig; bestval_sc=best_sc
      for val in vals:
         val=float(np.clip(val,0,1000))
         a[i]=val
         sc=_score_internal(a)
         if finite and sc<bestval_sc:
            bestval_sc=sc; bestv=val
      a[i]=bestv
      if bestval_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
         best_sc=bestval_sc; improved=True
    if not improved: break
  # normalize maybe
  return _normalize(a), best_sc
```
This does `a[i]=orig` initially because score function normalizes; If we set one coord to val and leave others sum not1, score invariant no need resubtract from others. But after trying multiple vals, we keep a with best val (others original); sum changes, score normalized. This is equivalent to varying ratio. Good. However, after coordinate change, sum of a is not 1; for next coordinate, original vector has sum maybe !=1. Score function normalizes; it's okay. But values may accumulate scaling? Since we set coordinate to absolute val relative to others not renormalized, the scale is arbitrary. If sum becomes very large or small, conv values scale but score finite. To maintain numerical scale, after each coordinate change we can normalize a so sum=1. Let's do: after setting bestv, `s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s`. This keeps scale. But during trial vals, score_internal normalizes, so best val selection unaffected by global scale. After selection normalize. This is equivalent to changing one coordinate ratio while sum1. Good.

When trying vals, if a sum not1 before? We maintain sum1. For a trial, set a[i]=val and not renorm; score_internal normalizes. Good. The set of candidate vals should be relative to original sum1. Good.

Could add a small golden section refinement around best multiplier: If best multiplier not endpoint, sample [0.9,1.1]*bestv maybe. Optional.

Use internal score every trial; for n=1024, 8 evals*1024*2 passes ~16k; okay if time. But if L=1024 internal conv FFT length 1024 (conv same) cost ~? maybe 0.05ms? 16k*0.1ms=1.6s plus Python overhead. Fine.

But coordinate polish changes one coord at a time; score uses full convolution each time. It may be slower than L-BFGS. Use if time >30.

```
def _lp_direction(f, M, stop=None):
 n=f.size
 A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
 for k in range(2*n-1):
   j0 = max(0,k-n+1); j1 = min(n-1,k)
   if j0<=j1:
      js = np.arange(j0,j1+1)
      A[k, js] = f[k-js]
 c = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,1)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
 if status !=0: return None
 g=c.x; finite clip; if sum<1e-12: None; return g/g.sum()
```
Need if `linprog` method highs not available? options. If fails, return.

def _best_t_line(f,g):
 cf=conv_same(f); cg=conv_same(g); cfs=conv_cross(f,g)
 # Actually for linear covect (1-t)f+tg, coefficient expression:
 # (1-t)^2 cf + 2t(1-t)cfs + t^2 cg = cf + (2cfs-2cf)t + (cf-2cfs+cg)t^2
 b = 2*(cfs-cf); c = cf - 2*cfs + cg
 ts=np.linspace(0,1,2048)
 vals=np.maximum(cf,0)??? But can negative due FFT; use cf np.maximum(cf,0) and cfs/cg max. But expression can have negative due numeric; for max, use as is? Use `cf = np.maximum(cf,0); cfs = np.maximum(cfs,0); cg = np.maximum(cg,0)` for stability. Maximum of combination may be slightly negative at inactive; okay.

 vals = np.max(vals, axis=1)
 i=argmin; refine: lo,hi around; 4096 local.
 return float(best_t)
```

def _lp_update(f0, stop, seed, max_iters=3):
 f=_normalize(f0); best=f; best_sc=...
 for it in range(max_iters):
   if time>stop-10: break
   M=conv_same(f).max()
   if M<=0:break
   g=_lp_direction(f,M)
   if g is None: break
   t=_best_t_line(f,g)
   cts=set([0,1,t, max(0,t-0.1), min(1,t+0.1), 0.25,0.5,0.75])
   best_new=f; best_new_sc=best_sc
   for tt in cts: new=(1-tt)f+tt*g; if finite sc<... 
   # if g itself score better? t=1 included
   if best_new_sc < best_sc - 1e-12: f=best_new; best_sc=...
   else: break
 return best,best_sc
```

` _fft_len`:
```
def _fft_len(m):
 m=int(max(1,m)); v=_fftcache.get(m); if v is None: v=int(next_fast_len(m)); _fftcache[m]=v; return v
```

`_as_finite_a`:
```
def _clean(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
 if a.size==0: return np.array([],dtype=float)
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
 a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
 return a
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a, L=None):
 a=_clean(a)
 if a.size==0:
   if L is None: L=256
   return np.ones(L)/L
 s=float(a.sum())
 if not math.isfinite(s) or s<1e-12:
   return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
 return a/s
```
For candidate, if sum <0.01 but finite, we scale to 1? It may be too small. Use if s >0: a/s. This makes sum1; if a entries become >1000? If original sum tiny, entries maybe also tiny; scaling could exceed 1000 if one dominates >1e3*sum? But our sequences sum ~1. For safety:
```
out=a/s
if out.max()>1000: out=np.clip(out,0,1000); s2=sum; out=out/s2 if s2>0
```
Not needed.

`_conv_same` direct threshold 80:
```
def _conv_same(a):
 n=int(a.size)
 if n==0: return np.array([],float)
 if n <= 80:
   c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
 else:
   L=_fft_len(2*n-1)
   fa=rfft(a,L)
   c=irfft(fa*fa,L,backend=None?) # irfft param? just irfft
   c=c[:2*n-1]
 c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
 return c
```
Scipy irfft has `s` parameter; `irfft(fa*fa, L)`? In scipy.fft, `irfft(a, n=None, ...)`. We should call `irfft(fa*fa, n=L)`.  For length 2n-1, rfft returns L//2+1 complex. Good.

`_conv_cross`:
```
def _conv_cross(a,b):
 n1,n2=int(a.size), int(b.size)
 if n1==0 or n2==0: return empty
 if min(n1,n2) <= 80:
   c=np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')
 else:
   L=_fft_len(n1+n2-1)
   c=irfft(rfft(a,L,n? )? Actually rfft(a,L); c=irfft(rfft(a,L)*rfft(b,L), L)[:n1+n2-1]
 c=np.maximum(c,0)
 return c
```
For same cross, if n1==n2, could call same.

`_valid_conv`:
```
def _valid_conv(w,a):
 n=int(a.size)
 if n==0: return empty
 if n <= 80:
   c=np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')
   return c[n-1:2*n-1]
 L=_fft_len(3*n-2)
 cw=w if w.size==2*n-1 else np.resize? assume.
 ar=a[::-1]
 c=irfft(rfft(cw,L)*rfft(ar,L), L)[:3*n-2]
 c=np.maximum(c[ n-1:2*n-1 ],0.0)
 return c
```
Symmetrize w? w may have tiny asym due FFT? c is not symmetric generally, but `c` from conv(a,a) can be slightly negative; w positive. valid formula uses w as given. Fine.

Potential FFT errors in valid convolution: w length 2n-1, ar length n. output slice [n-1:2n-1]. Good.

`_score_internal`:
```
def _score_internal(a):
 a=_clean(a)
 if a.size==0: return inf
 S=float(a.sum())
 if not finite or S<1e-300: return inf
 n=a.size
 c=_conv_same(a)
 M=float(c.max())
 if not finite or M<0: return inf
 return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
If S not 1, M scales with a; okay. For speed, maybe if S close1 use direct.

`_exact_score`:
```
def _exact_score(a):
 try:
   fn=globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
   if fn is None: return inf
   a=_clean(a)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   # ensure sum positive reasonable
   S=a.sum()
   if S<0.01: a=a/max(S,1e-12)?
   # To avoid huge list? 
   lst=[float(x) for x in a]
   s=float(fn(lst))
   return s if math.isfinite(s) else inf
 except Exception: return inf
```
Evaluator clips; if we pass unnormalized sum<0.01, inf. Our a sum1. If S<1 (maybe after coordinate normalization? sum1). Good.

`_make_inits`:
Need use rng. We'll limit to 14.
```
def _make_inits(L, parent, best, extras=None, rng=None, count=14):
 if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
 res=[]
 def add(a):
   a=_normalize(a, L)
   if a.size!=L: return
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
   # avoid duplicates: compare max abs to existing? Could use key rounded if L<=256 else skip
   if len(res)<count:
      # simple duplicate check for small:
      dup=False
      for b in res:
         if b.size==L and np.max(np.abs(b-b.reshape?)) <1e-12: dup=True; break
      if not dup: res.append(a)
 if parent is not None: add(_interp(parent,L))
 if best is not None: add(_interp(best,L))
 if extras: for a in extras[:2]: add(_interp(a,L))
 # perturb parent
 if parent is not None:
   p=_interp(parent,L)
   x=np.linspace(-1,1,L)
   add(p*(1+0.08*x))
   add(p*(1+0.03*rng.normal(size=L)))
 add(np.ones(L))
 x=(np.arange(L)+0.5)/float(L)
 # ramps
 for p in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0):
   add(np.power(x+1e-6,p))
   add(np.power(1-x+1e-6,p))
 # exp skew
 for c in (1.0,3.0,8.0):
   add(np.exp(c*(x-0.5)))
   add(np.exp(-c*(x-0.5)))
 # tanh
 for k in (1.0,3.0,9.0):
   for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.85):
     add(1.0 + amp*np.tanh(k*(x-0.5)*2.0))
 # cos
 for freq in (1,2):
   for amp in (0.2,0.5):
     add(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*(x-0.5)))
 # random smooth
 for _ in range(2):
   v=rng.normal(size=L)
   w=max(2,L//16)
   k=np.ones(w)//w
   v=np.convolve(v,k,mode='same')
   add(0.25 + np.abs(v))
 add(0.5*rng.exponential(size=L))
 return res
```
This may exceed count; `add` stops at count. Good. But many inits duplicate; duplicate check max O(count² L) small. If L=1024 and count14, 14²*1024=200k, okay.

Maybe parent perturb `p*(1+0.03*rng.normal)` can create negative if p small; _normalize clips negative to 0. Good. Use `np.abs`? no.

`_softmax_z` helper:
```
def _z_to_a(zv, anchor, n):
 z=np.empty(n)
 if anchor>0: z[:anchor]=zv[:anchor]
 z[anchor]=0.0
 if anchor+1<n: z[anchor+1:]=zv[anchor:]
 m=z.max()
 ex=np.exp(z-m)
 Z=ex.sum()
 if not finite or Z<=0: return uniform
 a=ex/Z
 return a
```
Ensure zv length n-1. If anchor>0 and zv shorter? no.

In `fg`, `z` allocation each call can be optimized by prealloc? But fine.

def _process_length(L, stop, parent, best_seq, best_score, rng, per_beta=160, beta_count=5, max_trials=3, profile_list=None):
  if time>stop: return best_seq,best_score
  inits=_make_inits(L, parent, best_seq, profile_list, rng, count=16)
  scored=[]
  for a in inits:
    sc=_score_internal(a)
    if finite: scored.append((sc,a))
  scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
  if not scored: return
  # include current best_seq if same length but not in list? add.
  if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size==L:
    scored.append((_score_internal(best_seq), best_seq.copy()))
    scored.sort()
  # top trials
  trials=[]
  for sc,a in scored:
    if len(trials) >= max_trials: break
    if sc >= best_score + 0.25 and len(trials)>0: break # skip far
    trials.append(a)
  for idx,a in enumerate(trials):
    now=time.monotonic(); if now>stop-5: break
    share = max(10.0, (stop - now)/(len(trials)-idx))
    substop = min(stop - 3.0, now + share*0.9)
    arr,_ = _smooth_refine(a, substop, per_beta=per_beta, beta_count=beta_count)
    # try exact best
    # But _process_length needs nonlocal best. We can return arrays and scores to propose, which updates.
```

Implementation:
```
def _improve_length(L, stop, per_beta, beta_count, max_trials):
  if L<=0 or time>stop: return
  inits = _make_inits(L, parent, best_seq, profile_list, rng, count=16)
  if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size==L: inits.append(best_seq.copy())
  if parent is not None and parent.size==L: inits.append(_normalize(parent)) # parent already
  scored=[]
  for a in inits:
    sc=_score_internal(a)
    if math.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
  scored.sort(key=lambda z:z[0])
  trials=[]
  for sc,a in scored:
    if len(trials)>=max_trials: break
    if trials and sc > best_score + 0.35: break
    trials.append((sc,a))
  # If no trials, return
  if not trials: return
  local_best=None; local_sc=inf
  for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(trials):
    if time>stop-8: break
    remaining = len(trials)-idx
    substop = min(stop-4, time.monotonic() + max(8, (stop-time.monotonic())/remaining*0.85))
    arr, sc_arr = _smooth_refine(a, substop, per_beta=per_beta, beta_count=beta_count)
    _try_candidate(arr)
    if sc_arr < local_sc: local_best=arr; local_sc=sc_arr
  # coordinate polish on local_best if time and length <= L (any)
  if local_best is not None and time.monotonic() < stop-20 and local_best.size <= 1024:
    pol,_ = _coordinate_polish(local_best, stop-5, seed=int(rng.integers(1,1000)), max_passes=1)
    _try_candidate(pol)
    if _score_internal(pol) < local_sc: local_best=pol; local_sc=...
  # LP if L<=320 and time
  if local_best is not None and local_best.size <= 320 and time.monotonic() < stop-60:
     lp,_ = _lp_update(local_best, stop-25, max_iters=2)
     _try_candidate(lp)
```
Local coordinate before LP? LP can make big moves; coordinate after LP maybe. We'll do LP then coordinate final in propose. But coordinate on local before LP okay. Maybe coordinate after LP better. Move coordinate to final global phase to refine best_seq.

In `_improve_length`, do LP then return; final coordinate on global best later.

Need `_lp_update` nested? outside uses functions.

But many. Keep simple: After short L=96, append `local_best`. After main L=256, append best if improved? `_make_inits` for 512 already includes `best_seq` interpolation, so profile not needed if global best improved. ```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
 t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s)); hard=t0+budget
 reserve = max(15.0, 0.05*budget)
 deadline = hard - reserve

 best_seq = parent if parent else _normalize(ones(256))
 best_score = _exact_score(best_seq) or internal.
 # if parent missing or score inf, fallback uniform and score.
 rng=...
 def _try_candidate...
 # if budget tiny <80: polish and return
 if budget <80:
    arr,_=_coordinate_polish(best_seq, deadline-2, seed=1)
    _try_candidate(arr)
    return _finalize()
 # maybe short profile if parent size not small

 if budget>200:
   Lshort = 96 if parent is None or parent.size>128 else max(32,parent.size//2)
   best_aux = best_seq
   ... run _improve_length(Lshort, short_stop, per_beta=50,beta_count=4,max_trials=4, no LP? maybe skip LP because small)
   # update best
 # phase main at parent length

 if parent:
    Lmain = parent.size
 else: Lmain=256
 _improve_length(Lmain, main_stop, per_beta=220, beta_count=6, max_trials=3)
 # phase next length: 512

   _improve_length(512, min(deadline-120, time+200), per_beta=180,beta_count=5,max_trials=2)
 # phase larger: 768 or 1024
 if time < deadline - 120:
   L3=768 if best_seq.size<=512 else 1024 maybe

    _try_candidate(arr)

 return _finalize()

def _z_from_a(a, anchor):

`_conv_same`:
```
def _conv_same(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 80:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode='full')
        return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = _fft_len(2*n - 1)
    fa = rfft(a, L)
    c = irfft(fa * fa, L, )[:2*n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
```
Scipy.fft.irfft signature: `irfft(x, n=None, axes=-1, norm='backward', overwrite_x=False, workers=None, *, backend=None)`. Passing L as positional works. Good.

`_conv_cross` for n1/n2: if min n <=80 direct. But for n1=2n-1, n2=n in valid via `np.convolve` may do direct; we have separate. For line search, cross length n,n; n>80 FFT.
```
def _conv_cross(a,b):
 n1,n2=int(a.size),int(b.size)
 if n1==0 or n2==0: return empty
 if min(n1,n2) <= 80:
   c=np.convolve(a,b,mode='full'); return np.maximum(c,0)
 L=_fft_len(n1+n2-1)
 c=irfft(rfft(a,L)*rfft(b,L), L)[:n1+n2-1]
 return np.maximum(c,0)
```
No need if a=b.

def _valid_conv(w,a):
 n=int(a.size)
 if n==0: return np.array([], float)

   c=np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')

 c=irfft(rfft(w,L)*rfft(a[::-1],L), L)[:3*n-2]
 out=c[n-1:2*n-1]
 return np.maximum(out,0)
```

```
def _coordinate_polish(a0, stop, seed=1, max_passes=2, mults=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25,1.5,2.0,3.0)):
 a=_normalize(a0).copy(); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(a)
 if a.size<2: return best,best_sc
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 n=a.size
 default=1.0/(4.0*n)
 for _ in range(max_passes):
   improved=False
   order=np.random.default_rng(seed + _).permutation(n)? Use rng.
   for i in order:
      if time.monotonic() > stop: break
      orig=float(a[i])
      vals={0.0, orig, default, default*2.0, default*4.0}
      for m in mults: vals.add(orig*float(m))
      if orig < default:
         for m in (1,2,4,8): vals.add(default*float(m))
      bestv=orig; bsc=best_sc
      for val in vals:
        val=float(np.clip(val,0,1000))
        a[i]=val
        sc=_score_internal(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<bsc:
           bsc=sc; bestv=val
      a[i]=bestv
      s=a.sum()
      if s>0: a=a/s
      if bsc < best_sc - 1e-14:
         best_sc=bsc; best=a.copy(); improved=True
   if not improved: break
 return best,best_sc
```

def _lp_direction(f, M, stop):
 if linprog is None: return None

 if n<2 or n>384: return None

    if j0<=j1:
       # Use vector js to avoid loop? A[k,j0:j1+1] = f[k-j1:k-j0+1][::-1]?
       A[k,j0:j1+1] = f[k-j1:k-j0+1][::-1]
    if time.monotonic()>stop: return None

   return None

 s=g.sum()
 if s<1e-12: return None
 return g/s

def _lp_update(f0, stop, max_iters=2):
 f=_normalize(f0); best=f.copy(); best_sc=_score_internal(f)

   if time.monotonic()>stop-15 or linprog is None or f.size>384: break
   c=_conv_same(f); M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0: break
   g=_lp_direction(f,M,stop-10)
   if g is None: break
   t=_best_t_line(f,g,stop)
   cands={0.0,1.0,float(t),max(0,float(t)-0.1),min(1,float(t)+0.1),0.25,0.5,0.75}
   best_tt=float(t); bsc=best_sc
   for tt in sorted(cands):
     if time.monotonic()>stop: break
     new=(1-tt)*f+tt*g
     if new.size: 
       s=new.sum(); if s>0: new=new/s
       sc=_score_internal(new)
       if finite and sc<bsc: bsc=sc; best_tt=tt
   if bsc < best_sc - 1e-13:
      new=(1-best_tt)*f+best_tt*g; new=_normalize(new); f=new; best=new.copy(); best_sc=bsc
   else: break
 return best,best_sc

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()

    scale = min(1.0, max(0.25, budget/1300.0))

    # short profile

```
def _make_inits(L, parent, best, rng, count=16):
    L=int(L)
    if L<2: L=2
    res=[]
    def add(a):
       if len(res)>=count: return
       a=_normalize(a, L)
       if a.size!=L: return
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
       for b in res:
          if np.max(np.abs(a-b)) < 1e-10:
             return
       res.append(a)
    if parent is not None:
       add(_interp(parent,L))
       p=_interp(parent,L)
       x=np.linspace(-1,1,L)
       add(p*(1+0.08*x))
       add(p*(1+0.04*rng.normal(size=L)))
    if best is not None and best.size != L:
       add(_interp(best,L))
    add(np.ones(L))
    ...
    return res
```

`_z_to_a`:
```
def _z_to_a(v, anchor, n):
 v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
 if v.size != n-1: # truncate/pad
    tmp=np.zeros(n-1); m=min(v.size,n-1); tmp[:m]=v[:m]; v=tmp
 z=np.empty(n)
 if anchor>0: z[:anchor]=v[:anchor]
 z[anchor]=0.0
 if anchor+1<n: z[anchor+1:]=v[anchor:]
 m=float(np.max(z))
 ex=np.exp(z-m)
 Z=float(ex.sum())
 if not finite or Z<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
 a=ex/Z
 return np.where(finite,a,0)
```

    def _run_length(L, stop, per_beta, beta_count, max_trials, use_lp):
        L = int(max(2, L))
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
            return
        inits = _make_inits(L, parent, best_seq, rng, count=16)
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == L:
            inits.append(best_seq.copy())
        scored = []
        seen_set = []
        for a in inits:
            if a is None: continue
            sc = _score_internal(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, a))
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        if not scored:
            return
        trials=[]
        for sc,a in scored:
            if len(trials) >= max_trials: break
            if trials and sc > best_score + 0.4:
                break
            trials.append(a)
        if not trials:
            trials = [scored[0][1]]
        local_best = None
        local_sc = math.inf
        for idx, a in enumerate(trials):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > stop - 6.0: break
            rem = max(1, len(trials)-idx)
            substop = min(stop - 2.0, now + max(5.0, (stop - now) / rem * 0.9))
            arr, sc_arr = _smooth_refine(a, substop, per_beta=per_beta, beta_count=beta_count)
            _try_candidate(arr)
            if sc_arr < local_sc:
                local_best = arr
                local_sc = sc_arr
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > stop - 10.0: break
        if use_lp and local_best is not None and local_best.size <= 320 and time.monotonic() < stop - 70.0:
            lp, sc_lp = _lp_update(local_best, stop - 25.0, max_iters=2)
            _try_candidate(lp)
            if sc_lp < local_sc:
                local_best = lp
                local_sc = sc_lp
        if local_best is not None and time.monotonic() < stop - 25.0 and local_best.size <= 1024:
            pol, sc_pol = _coordinate_polish(local_best, stop - 2.0, seed=int(rng.integers(0, 10**9)), max_passes=1)
            _try_candidate(pol)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


class _Timeout(Exception):
    pass


_fft_cache = {}


def _fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    v = _fft_cache.get(m)
    if v is None:
        v = int(next_fast_len(m))
        _fft_cache[m] = v
    return v


def _clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _normalize(a, fallback_len=None):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        n = int(fallback_len) if fallback_len is not None else 256
        n = max(2, n)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    return a


def _conv_same(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 80:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
        return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    fa = rfft(a, L)
    c = irfft(fa * fa, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _conv_cross(a, b):
    n1 = int(a.size)
    n2 = int(b.size)
    if n1 == 0 or n2 == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if min(n1, n2) <= 80:
        c = np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")
        return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = _fft_len(n1 + n2 - 1)
    c = irfft(rfft(a, L) * rfft(b, L), L)[: n1 + n2 - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _valid_conv(w, a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 80:
        c = np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode="full")
        out = c[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
        return np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    L = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    c = irfft(rfft(w, L) * rfft(a[::-1], L), L)[: 3 * n - 2]
    out = c[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _score_internal(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return math.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    c = _conv_same(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if fn is None:
            return math.inf
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return math.inf
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        val = float(fn(lst))
        return val if math.isfinite(val) else math.inf
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
    except Exception:
        return None
    if h is None:
        return None
    a = _normalize(h)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    return a


def _interp_sequence(seq, L):
    L = int(max(2, L))
    seq = _normalize(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L) / float(L)
    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.ones(L) / float(L)
    if seq.size == L:
        return seq
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    v = np.interp(x1, x0, seq)
    return _normalize(v)


def _make_inits(L, parent, best, rng, count=16):
    L = int(max(2, L))
    res = []

    def add(a):
        if len(res) >= count:
            return
        a = _normalize(a, L)
        if a.size != L:
            return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        for b in res:
            if np.max(np.abs(a - b)) < 1e-11:
                return
        res.append(a)

    if parent is not None:
        p = _interp_sequence(parent, L)
        add(p)
        x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, L)
        add(p * (1.0 + 0.08 * x))
        add(p * (1.0 + 0.04 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=L)))

    if best is not None:
        add(_interp_sequence(best, L))

    add(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))

    x = (np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(L)

    for p in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        add(np.power(x + 1e-6, p))
        add(np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-6, p))

    for c in (1.0, 3.0, 8.0):
        add(np.exp(c * (x - 0.5)))
        add(np.exp(-c * (x - 0.5)))

    for k in (1.0, 3.0, 9.0):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.85):
            add(1.0 + amp * np.tanh(k * (x - 0.5) * 2.0))

    for freq in (1, 2):
        for amp in (0.2, 0.5):
            add(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * (x - 0.5)))

    for _ in range(2):
        v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=L)
        w = max(2, L // 16)
        kernel = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
        v = np.convolve(v, kernel, mode="same")
        add(0.25 + np.abs(v))

    add(0.5 * rng.exponential(1.0, size=L))
    return res


def _z_from_a(a, anchor):
    n = int(a.size)
    v = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    base = max(float(a[anchor]), 1e-200)
    idx = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i == anchor:
            continue
        r = float(a[i]) / base
        if r <= 0.0:
            val = -50.0
        else:
            val = math.log(r)
        v[idx] = max(-50.0, min(50.0, val))
        idx += 1
    return v


def _z_to_a(v, anchor, n):
    n = int(n)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != n - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(v.size, n - 1)
        tmp[:m] = v[:m]
        v = tmp
    v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor > 0:
        z[:anchor] = v[:anchor]
    z[anchor] = 0.0
    if anchor + 1 < n:
        z[anchor + 1:] = v[anchor:]
    m = float(np.max(z))
    ex = np.exp(z - m)
    Z = float(ex.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = ex / Z
    return np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)


def _smooth_refine(a0, stop, per_beta=160, beta_count=5):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        sc = _score_internal(a)
        return a, sc
    if not math.isfinite(stop) or time.monotonic() >= stop - 2.0:
        return a, _score_internal(a)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_internal(a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1.5

    if n > 1:
        rev = a[::-1]
        if float(np.max(np.abs(a - rev))) <= 1e-6 * max(1e-12, float(a.max())):
            a2 = a * (1.0 + 0.03 * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n))
            a2 = _normalize(a2)
            sc2 = _score_internal(a2)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                a = a2
                best = a.copy()
                best_sc = sc2

    cur = a.copy()
    score_for_m = best_sc if math.isfinite(best_sc) else 1.5
    m_est = max(float(score_for_m) / (2.0 * n), 1e-6)
    b0 = max(4.0, min(5000.0, 0.5 / m_est))
    betas = []
    b = b0
    for _ in range(max(1, int(beta_count))):
        betas.append(min(b, 40000.0))
        b *= 4.0

    nvar = n - 1
    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * nvar

    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stop - 1.0 or nvar == 0:
            break
        anchor = int(np.argmax(cur))
        v0 = _z_from_a(cur, anchor)

        def func(v, _beta=beta, _anchor=anchor, _n=n):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 0.3:
                raise _Timeout
            v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(_n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)

            z = np.empty(_n, dtype=np.float64)
            if _anchor > 0:
                z[:_anchor] = v[:_anchor]
            z[_anchor] = 0.0
            if _anchor + 1 < _n:
                z[_anchor + 1:] = v[_anchor:]

            mz = float(np.max(z))
            ex = np.exp(z - mz)
            Z = float(ex.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
                return 1e100, np.zeros(_n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            a = ex / Z

            c = _conv_same(a)
            M = float(c.max())
            if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
                return 0.0, np.zeros(_n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

            cw = _beta * (c - M)
            cw = np.clip(cw, -745.0, 0.0)
            w = np.exp(cw)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                w = np.ones_like(c) / float(c.size)
                s = float(c.size)
            F = M + math.log(s) / _beta

            d = w / s
            ga = 2.0 * _valid_conv(d, a)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)):
                ga = np.zeros(_n, dtype=np.float64)
            dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
            gf = a * (ga - dot)

            grad = np.empty(_n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            if _anchor > 0:
                grad[:_anchor] = gf[:_anchor]
            if _anchor + 1 < _n:
                grad[_anchor:] = gf[_anchor + 1:]
            grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
            return float(F), grad

        def callback(k, f_k, g_k):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 0.3:
                raise _Timeout

        maxfun = max(25, int(per_beta))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                func,
                v0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(50, maxfun),
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except _Timeout:
            res = None
        except Exception:
            res = None

        cand_v = v0
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            cand_v = res.x

        cand_v = np.clip(np.asarray(cand_v, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), -50.0, 50.0)
        a_opt = _z_to_a(cand_v, anchor, n)
        sc_opt = _score_internal(a_opt)

        if math.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc:
            best = a_opt.copy()
            best_sc = sc_opt
        if math.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_sc + 0.02:
            cur = a_opt.copy()

    return best, best_sc


def _coordinate_polish(a0, stop, seed=1, max_passes=2):
    a = _normalize(a0).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, _score_internal(a)
    if not math.isfinite(stop):
        return a, _score_internal(a)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_internal(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    default = 1.0 / (4.0 * n)
    mults = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0)

    for _p in range(max_passes):
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop:
                break
            orig = float(a[i])
            vals = {0.0, orig, default, 2.0 * default, 4.0 * default}
            for m in mults:
                vals.add(orig * float(m))
            if orig < default:
                for m in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
                    vals.add(default * float(m))

            bestv = orig
            bsc = best_sc
            for val in vals:
                val = float(np.clip(val, 0.0, 1000.0))
                a[i] = val
                sc = _score_internal(a)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < bsc:
                    bsc = sc
                    bestv = val

            a[i] = bestv
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
                a = a / s

            if bsc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = bsc
                best = a.copy()
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            break
    return best, best_sc


def _best_t_line(f, g):
    cf = _conv_same(f)
    cfs = _conv_cross(f, g)
    cg = _conv_same(g)
    cf = np.maximum(cf, 0.0)
    cfs = np.maximum(cfs, 0.0)
    cg = np.maximum(cg, 0.0)

    b = 2.0 * (cfs - cf)
    c = cf - 2.0 * cfs + cg

    ts = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 2048)
    vals = np.max(cf[None, :] + b[None, :] * ts[:, None] + c[None, :] * ts[:, None] ** 2, axis=1)
    i = int(np.argmin(vals))
    lo = ts[max(0, i - 16)]
    hi = ts[min(len(ts) - 1, i + 16)]
    ts2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 1024)
    vals2 = np.max(cf[None, :] + b[None, :] * ts2[:, None] + c[None, :] * ts2[:, None] ** 2, axis=1)
    return float(ts2[int(np.argmin(vals2))])


def _lp_direction(f, M, stop):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 2 or n > 320:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(n - 1, k)
        if j0 <= j1:
            A[k, j0:j1 + 1] = f[k - j1:k - j0 + 1][::-1]
        if time.monotonic() > stop:
            return None

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0 or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_update(f0, stop, max_iters=2):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = _score_internal(f)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if (
            linprog is None
            or f.size < 2
            or f.size > 320
            or not math.isfinite(stop)
            or now >= stop - 70.0
        ):
            break

        c = _conv_same(f)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        g = _lp_direction(f, M, stop - 60.0)
        if g is None:
            break

        t0 = _best_t_line(f, g)
        cands = {
            0.0,
            1.0,
            float(t0),
            max(0.0, float(t0) - 0.1),
            min(1.0, float(t0) + 0.1),
            0.25,
            0.5,
            0.75,
        }

        best_t = float(t0)
        bsc = best_sc
        for tt in sorted(cands):
            if time.monotonic() > stop:
                break
            tt = float(np.clip(tt, 0.0, 1.0))
            new = (1.0 - tt) * f + tt * g
            new = _normalize(new)
            sc = _score_internal(new)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < bsc:
                bsc = sc
                best_t = tt

        if bsc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            new = (1.0 - best_t) * f + best_t * g
            new = _normalize(new)
            f = new
            best = new.copy()
            best_sc = bsc
        else:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0
    budget = max(1.0, budget)

    hard = t0 + budget
    if budget <= 30.0:
        deadline = hard - 0.8
    else:
        reserve = max(8.0, 0.05 * budget)
        deadline = hard - reserve

    parent = _get_parent()
    if parent is None:
        fallback = _normalize(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
    else:
        fallback = parent.copy()

    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _score_internal(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    exact_recheck_count = [0]

    def _try_candidate(a):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        sc_int = _score_internal(a)
        if not math.isfinite(sc_int):
            return

        if math.isfinite(best_score):
            allow_recheck = False
            if (
                a.size <= 512
                and sc_int < best_score + 3e-4
                and exact_recheck_count[0] < 160
            ):
                allow_recheck = True
            if not allow_recheck and sc_int >= best_score - 1e-9:
                return

        exact_recheck_count[0] += 1
        sc = _exact_score(a)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            sc = sc_int
        if math.isfinite(sc) and (not math.isfinite(best_score) or sc < best_score - 1e-12):
            best_seq = a.copy()
            best_score = sc
        elif not math.isfinite(best_score) and math.isfinite(sc_int):
            best_seq = a.copy()
            best_score = sc_int

    def _phase_stop(alloc, reserve_after):
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = deadline - max(0.0, reserve_after)
        cand = min(limit, now + max(5.0, float(alloc)))
        if cand < now + 4.0:
            return min(deadline, now + 2.0)
        return cand

    def _run_length(L, stop, per_beta, beta_count, max_trials, use_lp):
        L = int(max(2, L))
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
            return

        inits = _make_inits(L, parent, best_seq, rng, count=16)
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == L:
            inits.append(best_seq.copy())

        scored = []
        for a in inits:
            if a is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_internal(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, a))
        if not scored:
            return
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

        trials = []
        for sc, a in scored:
            if len(trials) >= max_trials:
                break
            if trials and math.isfinite(best_score) and sc > best_score + 0.45:
                break
            trials.append(a)
        if not trials:
            trials = [scored[0][1]]

        local_best = None
        local_sc = math.inf

        for idx, a in enumerate(trials):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > stop - 6.0:
                break
            rem = max(1, len(trials) - idx)
            substop = min(stop - 2.0, now + max(5.0, (stop - now) / rem * 0.9))
            arr, sc_arr = _smooth_refine(a, substop, per_beta=per_beta, beta_count=beta_count)
            _try_candidate(arr)
            if sc_arr < local_sc:
                local_best = arr
                local_sc = sc_arr
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 8.0:
                break

        if (
            use_lp
            and local_best is not None
            and local_best.size <= 320
            and time.monotonic() < stop - 75.0
        ):
            lp, sc_lp = _lp_update(local_best, stop - 25.0, max_iters=2)
            _try_candidate(lp)
            if sc_lp < local_sc:
                local_best = lp
                local_sc = sc_lp

        if (
            local_best is not None
            and time.monotonic() < stop - 25.0
            and local_best.size <= 1024
        ):
            pol, sc_pol = _coordinate_polish(
                local_best,
                stop - 2.0,
                seed=int(rng.integers(0, 1_000_000_000)),
                max_passes=1,
            )
            _try_candidate(pol)

    def _finalize():
        candidates = []
        for seq in (best_seq, fallback):
            if seq is None:
                continue
            s = _normalize(seq)
            if s.size == 0:
                continue
            s = np.clip(s, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ss = float(s.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(ss) or ss < 0.01:
                continue
            s = s / ss
            lst = [float(x) for x in s]
            sc = _exact_score(s)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                sc = _score_internal(s)
            candidates.append((sc if math.isfinite(sc) else math.inf, lst))

        if not candidates:
            u = [1.0 / 64.0] * 64
            try:
                if globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None) is not None:
                    evaluate_sequence(u)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return u

        candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        for sc, lst in candidates:
            if math.isfinite(sc) and lst:
                return lst
        return candidates[-1][1]

    if budget < 70.0:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            arr, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_seq, deadline - 1.0, seed=12345, max_passes=1)
            _try_candidate(arr)
        return _finalize()

    scale = max(0.25, min(1.0, budget / 1300.0))
    alloc_short = int(90 * scale) if budget > 260 else 0
    alloc_main = int(330 * scale)
    alloc_512 = int(260 * scale)
    alloc_768 = int(180 * scale)
    alloc_final = int(140 * scale)

    if parent is None:
        main_L = 256
    else:
        main_L = int(parent.size)
        if main_L < 64 or main_L > 1024:
            main_L = 256

    future_after_short = (
        max(alloc_main, 0.0)
        + max(alloc_512, 0.0)
        + max(alloc_768, 0.0)
        + max(alloc_final, 0.0)
    )
    if alloc_short > 15 and main_L > 128:
        stop = _phase_stop(alloc_short, future_after_short)
        Lshort = 96
        if parent is not None and parent.size <= 128:
            Lshort = max(32, int(parent.size))
        _run_length(Lshort, stop, per_beta=55, beta_count=4, max_trials=4, use_lp=False)

    if alloc_main > 15:
        stop = _phase_stop(alloc_main, alloc_512 + alloc_768 + alloc_final)
        _run_length(
            main_L,
            stop,
            per_beta=220,
            beta_count=6,
            max_trials=3,
            use_lp=(main_L <= 320),
        )

    if alloc_512 > 15:
        stop = _phase_stop(alloc_512, alloc_768 + alloc_final)
        _run_length(512, stop, per_beta=180, beta_count=5, max_trials=2, use_lp=False)

    if alloc_768 > 15:
        stop = _phase_stop(alloc_768, alloc_final)
        _run_length(768, stop, per_beta=160, beta_count=5, max_trials=2, use_lp=False)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - max(90.0, float(alloc_final) + 70.0):
        stop = min(deadline - alloc_final - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 190.0)
        if stop - time.monotonic() > 45.0:
            _run_length(1024, stop, per_beta=140, beta_count=5, max_trials=2, use_lp=False)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1200:
        arr, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_seq, deadline - 4.0, seed=987654321, max_passes=2)
        _try_candidate(arr)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        arr, _ = _smooth_refine(best_seq, deadline - 4.0, per_beta=260, beta_count=3)
        _try_candidate(arr)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1200:
        arr, _ = _coordinate_polish(best_seq, deadline - 2.0, seed=42424242, max_passes=1)
        _try_candidate(arr)

    return _finalize()
```
